Nov 03, 2026: XPENG Unveils Flagship SUVs and AI Innovations in Dubai Amid Middle East Expansion

XPENG, a leading global Physical AI company, showcased its latest flagship SUVs and introduced its Physical AI ecosystem during a significant event in Dubai. This initiative marks a crucial step in XPENG's expansion strategy within the Middle East and Africa (MEA) region, which is expected to accelerate rapidly. On the 3rd of November 2026, XPENG announced ambitious plans to enter all six Gulf Cooperation Council (GCC) markets by the end of this year, alongside plans for more extensive coverage in MEA next year.

Dr. Brian Gu, Vice Chairman and President of XPENG, emphasized the importance of the Middle East as a strategic market, highlighting the region's strong appetite for innovative technology. The company plans to broaden its reach across more than 20 countries within MEA by 2027, aiming to establish over 100 sales and service outlets. The unveiling of XPENG's Kunpeng Range Extender technology in its GX and G9L flagship SUVs aims to address the unique challenges of long distances and harsh climates prevalent in the region, providing a combined range of up to 1,210 kilometers without recharging.

The launch of the GX and G9L models comes with a notable reception, evidenced by over 4,000 orders across MEA, including 800 confirmed orders in the UAE alone. With the introduction of six new models specifically tailored for the Middle East expected within the next year, XPENG is ensuring its offerings are designed to meet regional demands. The GX is characterized by luxury features, including an 88-inch augmented reality head-up display and AI-powered seating, while the G9L focuses on a dynamic, family-friendly experience.

XPENG's expansion strategy includes new distribution agreements in Kuwait and Oman, which will facilitate full entry into the GCC markets. The company has already established a presence in various countries, including Qatar, Egypt, and Lebanon, and is recognized as a leading premium EV brand in Egypt. The GX and G9L models will be simultaneously launched across 11 markets, including Nigeria and Ghana, demonstrating XPENG's commitment to building a robust presence in the region.

Beyond electric vehicles, XPENG's vision encompasses a broader Physical AI ecosystem that includes robotaxis and humanoid robots. The company showcased its IRON humanoid robot at the Dubai event, with plans for initial overseas deliveries set for the first half of 2027. Additionally, XPENG is exploring opportunities for flying cars in the Middle East, where regulatory frameworks are beginning to take shape. This comprehensive approach positions XPENG as a key player in the future of mobility and smart living in the region.

Oct 28, 2026: XPeng Inc. Advances Autonomous Trials in China with VW Partnership

Volkswagen AG and Xpeng Inc. have secured approvals to trial autonomous driving systems on public roads in China, marking a significant step forward for the country's self-driving ambitions. On 28th October 2026, VW’s China unit announced it has commenced testing Level 3 technology in Hefei, aiming for deployment by next year. Concurrently, Xpeng Inc. revealed that its robotaxi division received authorization to conduct Level 4 testing across Guangzhou, initially accessible through invitation codes with plans for gradual expansion.

These recent approvals indicate a shift in regulatory stance following a high-profile incident involving Baidu Inc.'s self-driving vehicles in Wuhan earlier this year. The incident prompted the Chinese authorities to suspend new robotaxi licenses in April and launch a comprehensive safety review of the industry. Reports from Bloomberg News in July suggested that approvals were being reinstated, with Momenta Global Ltd. being one of the first companies to receive a permit. However, Xpeng Inc. and VW are among the few firms that have publicly confirmed their successful applications for road trials.

The Ministry of Industry and Information Technology, which regulates the autonomous driving sector, did not respond to inquiries regarding the latest approvals. As China continues to evolve its approach to self-driving technology, it faces the challenge of balancing rapid commercial deployment with ensuring public safety. Regulators have been proactive, revising standards related to various aspects of vehicle technology, including door handles, advanced driver-assistance systems, and battery specifications.

Both Xpeng Inc. and Volkswagen are strategically positioned to capitalize on this shift in regulatory sentiment, potentially paving the way for broader adoption of autonomous vehicles in the Chinese market. With these trials, the companies aim to demonstrate the viability and safety of their technologies to both regulators and consumers alike, addressing any lingering concerns stemming from previous incidents.

As the landscape for autonomous driving in China continues to evolve, Xpeng Inc. stands out as a key player in the sector. The developments around VW's collaboration and Xpeng's own advancements could significantly influence the future trajectory of self-driving technology in China, making these trials a focal point for investors and industry observers.

Oct 28, 2026: XPeng Advances Robot Production with Solid-State Battery Milestone

XPeng Inc. has announced a significant milestone in its robotics initiative, with the small-batch production of the solid-state battery for its IRON robot now complete. On 28th October 2026, during the Embodied Intelligence Robot Conference, Gu Jie, vice president of XPeng Group, revealed that the project's core power component has successfully rolled off the production line, indicating that the IRON robot is entering the sprint phase ahead of its standard operating procedure (SOP).

The completion of small-batch production is a critical step as SOP marks the transition from engineering validation to volume manufacturing. This development underscores XPeng's commitment to advancing its robotics segment, which is increasingly viewed as a vital part of the company's portfolio beyond its new energy vehicle production. XPeng Robotics has also made strides in establishing a robust supply chain, having conducted factory audits and identified key component suppliers, which positions the company well for large-scale production slated for the end of 2026.

The solid-state battery technology is a key differentiator for XPeng's IRON robot, touted as the first humanoid robot in the industry to utilize this advanced battery type. At the 2025 Tech Day, XPeng disclosed significant technical achievements, including a 78% volume utilization rate for the battery pack, which is markedly higher than traditional liquid battery packs, alongside a 35% reduction in weight. This innovation not only enhances the robot's performance but also aligns with XPeng's broader vision for energy-efficient solutions.

Chairman He Xiaopeng has previously indicated that XPeng is transitioning to in-house battery production, having invested in three battery cell companies. This strategic move is expected to provide a unified foundation for powering both the IRON robot and the company's electric vehicles, enhancing operational synergies. The IRON robot is anticipated to serve various functions in XPeng's retail and commercial environments starting in 2027, creating a feedback loop from production to deployment.

In the financial markets, XPeng's stock (09868.HK) saw a positive response, rising by HK$1.18, or approximately 3.35%, following the announcement. The rollout of the solid-state battery not only has implications for the IRON robot but also has potential ramifications for XPeng's electric vehicle offerings. However, the path to large-scale production will require overcoming challenges related to yield ramp-up and cost control, making the company's execution capabilities a focal point for investors as the end of 2026 approaches.

The company’s ongoing focus on research and development ensures that its intelligent mobility solutions meet the practical needs of everyday drivers.

With a growing product portfolio and an expanding sales and service network, XPeng is committed to making intelligent mobility accessible to more customers. Its approach, termed 'In Europe, For Europe, With Europe,' emphasizes local operations and adaptation of technologies to meet regional driving conditions.

XPeng's European research and development center in Munich is pivotal in tailoring its offerings to local customer preferences, further enhancing the brand's reputation. The recent debut of the G9L flagship SUV at the 2026 Paris Motor Show, coupled with an expanded lineup from three to six models in Germany alone, showcases XPeng's dedication to providing a diverse range of options for consumers. As the EV market evolves, XPeng's recognition in the USCALE study underscores its commitment to delivering intelligent technologies that align with the needs and expectations of its customers.

Oct 21, 2026: XPeng's NGP Achieves Smooth 30-Minute Loop, Drawing Investor Attention

XPeng Inc. showcased its next-generation driver-assistance technology in an impressive demonstration at the Paris Motor Show, completing a challenging 30-minute loop with zero driver interventions. On 23rd October 2026, host Spencer noted that the steering wheel of the XPeng vehicle, equipped with the NGP VLA 2.0 system, maintained 'perfectly smooth input,' a significant observation that signals the company's advancements in autonomous driving technology.

The route chosen for the demonstration was highly complex, running through dense urban areas filled with cyclists, pedestrians, and various traffic obstacles. Only XPeng and Tesla were granted permission to conduct such demonstrations on this specific course, which organizer Moog emphasized was representative of typical urban environments like New York City and London. This context is crucial, as it highlights the potential applicability of XPeng's technology in bustling metropolitan settings.

While the demonstration was a significant step forward, both Spencer and Moog were candid about the limitations of such a test. The 30-minute drive does not provide a comprehensive assessment of the system's capabilities, as it only showcases performance on a fixed route. Spencer acknowledged that more extensive testing would be necessary to draw definitive conclusions about the NGP system's reliability across diverse driving conditions.

The NGP system's ability to adapt to local driving conventions, such as allowing motorbikes and cyclists to queue ahead at intersections, indicates a level of localization not commonly seen in similar technologies. XPeng's driver-monitoring approach, which includes eye tracking and a requirement for driver engagement every 30 seconds, further emphasizes its commitment to safety, while also enhancing user experience.

Despite the positive feedback, gaps in capability remain. The system's sensor suite has not been definitively confirmed, and the absence of features such as voice-command navigation raises questions about its competitiveness in the market. As investors assess XPeng's position within the evolving landscape of autonomous driving, the company's ability to address these gaps will be critical in determining its future success.

Oct 12, 2026: Xpeng Plans European Production Boost to Meet Local Content Regulations

LONDON, Oct 12 (Reuters) - Chinese EV maker Xpeng (9868.HK) plans to expand its European production in order to comply with new European Union rules mandating minimum local content in electric cars, company vice president Yeqing Zheng told Reuters at the Paris car show.

The announcement comes as the EU tightens regulations aimed at boosting local manufacturing and reducing reliance on foreign supply chains, particularly from Asia. As part of its strategy, Xpeng is looking to bolster its presence in the European market, where demand for electric vehicles continues to grow.

Yeqing Zheng emphasized that establishing a more robust local production footprint is essential for Xpeng to remain competitive in the rapidly evolving EV landscape. The new local content rules, which are set to take effect in the coming years, require a significant percentage of components to be sourced from within the EU, pushing companies like Xpeng to adapt their manufacturing strategies accordingly.

Xpeng has already made significant inroads into the European market, launching several models that have garnered attention for their technology and pricing. However, the company recognizes that to succeed long-term, it must not only comply with regulations but also build trust with European consumers, who are increasingly favoring brands that demonstrate commitment to local economies.

The company did not disclose specific details about its planned expansion, including potential new factories or production lines. However, industry analysts suggest that Xpeng's proactive approach could position it favorably against competitors who may struggle to meet the new requirements, potentially opening up new opportunities for growth in the European EV sector.

SECTION 1 COMPANY OVERVIEW

1.1 Activities

XPeng Inc., a leading Chinese electric vehicle manufacturer listed on the U.S. ADR market, focuses on the design, development, manufacturing, and sales of smart electric vehicles equipped with advanced autonomous driving technologies and intelligent connectivity features. The company's core activities center on creating sustainable mobility solutions, including electric cars with AI-driven systems for enhanced safety and user experience. Among its fastest-growing segments are intelligent driving assistance technologies, which leverage AI and sensors for semi-autonomous features, and international market expansion, particularly in Europe and Asia, fueled by rising demand for eco-friendly transportation. Additionally, XPeng is advancing in battery management and smart ecosystem services, such as over-the-air updates and vehicle-to-everything connectivity, positioning it as a key innovator in the global EV industry. It is the International Depository Receipt (IDR) market's 12th largest Auto manufacturers company by market capitalization.

Oct 15, 2026: XPENG Expands Collaboration with Google to Bring Google Play into Its AI-Powered Cockpits

XPENG expands its collaboration with Google to offer Google Play directly inside of its AI-Powered cockpit.

Google Play combines hundreds of third-party apps across 100+ countries and regions withXPENG's intelligent capabilities for a richer, more localized in-car experience.

The integration is scheduled to go live in early 2027.

RAS AL KHAIMAH, UAE, Oct. 15, 2026 -- XPENG (NYSE: XPEV, HKEX: 9868), a leading Physical AI company, today announced a native integration with Google Play, marking an expansion of its strategic collaboration with Google.

XPENG will offer Google Play within its cars. This direct integration allows XPENG to offer its signature AI-Powered cockpit experience while giving users seamless, phone-optional access to a breadth of automotive-optimized apps available through Google Play. The integration is scheduled to go live in early 2027.

Through Google Play, XPENG users will have access to a thriving global ecosystem, with hundreds of apps from more than 100 countries built for the car screen across music and podcasts, video streaming, navigation, charging and parking, news, gaming while parked, and everyday lifestyle services. Whether relaxing at a charging stop or on the move, drivers can enjoy global services, alongside apps tailored to individual markets.

And because developers continuously publish new apps and updates through Google Play, the in-car experience will continually evolve without requiring major full-vehicle OTA updates.

Built on Android Automotive OS, the integration works seamlessly alongsideXPENG's intelligent cockpit capabilities, enabling drivers and passengers to use XPENG's multilingual AI-powered voice assistant to open and interact with downloaded apps.

Dr. Xianming Liu, Vice President of XPENG and Head of General Intelligence Center, said, "The best technology partnerships build momentum.

Press Release article 3 of 9, Source: XPENG, 304 words

Oct 08, 2026: XPENG Robotaxi Is Officially Named "XPENG YOYO"

On October 8, XPENG Robotaxi has officially announced its brand name "XPENG YOYO," opening its autonomous driving online registration to the public in China.

As China's first fully in-house developed Robotaxi to achieve pre-loaded mass production, XPENG Robotaxi is completing the leap from technical validation to commercial user operations.

GUANGZHOU, China, Oct. 8, 2026 -- XPENG's Robotaxi has officially announced its brand name "XPENG YOYO." On the same day, the XPENG YOYO autonomous driving online registration was opened to the public in China through invitation codes, and a dedicated page went live on XPENG's official website. This marks the transition from technical validation into a new stage of marketization and industrialization. Going forward, XPENG YOYO will operate with a clear brand identity and commercial positioning, providing operation services to users and partners.

Built on its in-house Turing AI chips, VLA2.0 large model, and AI infrastructure, XPENG is extending the AI capabilities accumulated in the automotive field further into Robotaxi, bridging L2 intelligent driver assistance and L4 autonomous driving. Based on this technology path, XPENG Robotaxi has rapidly completed the critical leap from R&D, mass production, and full-chain validation to public access within this year. This technology route, entirely different from that of traditional L4 companies, is accelerating toward commercial validation with real users and in real operation scenarios.

The English name "XPENG YOYO" was chosen for greater brand recognition in global markets. Short and easy to pronounce, the name maintains strong consistency in communication across various language environments, providing unified brand recognition for XPENG Robotaxi as it enters global markets in the future.

In May 2026, XPENG's mass-produced Robotaxi rolled off the production line in Guangzhou, completed the full chain from online ride-hailing to automated pickup and drop-off in July.

Press Release article 4 of 9, Source: XPENG, 306 words

Oct 07, 2026: XPENG G9L Completes First Production Trial in Europe

XPENG's Next-Gen AI Flagship SUV G9L has successfully completed its production trial at Magna's complete vehicle facility in Graz, Austria, becoming the fourth XPENG model to be produced in Europe after the G6, G9 and P7+.

Following the successful production trial, the first European G9L will travel 1,324 kilometers from Graz to Paris ahead of its global premiere at the Paris Motor Show on October 12, demonstrating the vehicle's safety, charging and driving dynamics under real-world conditions enabled by the cutting-edge AI technologies.

The G9L marks another step in XPENG's European localization strategy, combining local manufacturing in Europe with global vehicle development and standards.

GRAZ, Austria, Oct. 7, 2026 -- XPeng Inc. ("XPENG" or "the Company,"NYSE: XPEV; HKEX: 9868), a leading global Physical AI company, today announces that its Next-Gen AI Flagship SUV G9L has successfully completed its first production trial at Magna in Graz, Austria, marking a new milestone in its European manufacturing expansion.

The G9L is the fourth XPENG model to undergo production at the Austrian facility, where XPENG's G6, G9 and P7+ are actively being produced. The milestone comes one year after XPENG and Magna began local production in Graz and signals the latest expansion of their European manufacturing partnership.

The first pre-production G9L will now travel 1,324 kilometers from Graz to Paris ahead of its global launch at the Paris Motor Show on October 12, putting the vehicle through a real-world journey focused on charging, safety and driving performance before it is displayed at XPENG's stand.

The milestone builds on XPENG's accelerating growth in Europe. The company has delivered more than 100,000 vehicles overseas to date, including over 60,000 in Europe. In France alone, more than 6,000 vehicles have been delivered since entering the market two years ago.

Oct 01, 2026: XPENG Announces Vehicle Delivery Results for September and Third Quarter 2026

GUANGZHOU, China, Oct. 1, 2026 -- XPeng Inc. ("XPENG" or the "Company,"NYSE: XPEV and HKEX: 9868), a leading global Physical AI company, today announced its vehicle delivery results for September and the third quarter of 2026.

In September 2026, XPENG delivered 41,256 vehicles, representing a 5% increase over the prior month. Monthly deliveries of the XPENG L03 exceeded 10,000 units.

For the third quarter of 2026,XPENG delivered a total of 118,390 vehicles, marking a 15% increase over the previous quarter.

On September 17, 2026, XPENG officially launched the G9L, a premium flagship SUV featuring XPENG's latest technologies, in China. The model's global launch will take place in Paris, France, in October.

On September 22, 2026, XPENG began rolling out XOS 6.3.0 in China, powered by the latest version of its VLA 2.0 model.

As of September 30, 2026, XPENG's self-operated charging network in China covered more than 430 cities and comprised over 4,000 stations, including more than 3,510 ultra-fast charging stations, with over 22,200 charging piles in total. On September 15, XPENG opened its first-ever X-Energy megawatt ultra-fast charging station in Hong Kong.

XPENG's electric vehicles delivered from January to September 2026 are expected to reduce life-cycle greenhouse gas emissions by more than 4.14 million tons compared to internal combustion engine vehicles, equivalent to the carbon absorbed by 68.53 million young trees over 10 years.

GUANGZHOU, China, Sept. 23, 2026 -- From Walking to Interaction:XPENG's Humanoid Robot Demonstrate Advanced Intelligence On-Site

Core components must not only deliver stable performance, but also withstand the tests of mass production in consistency, cost, and delivery at scale.

UNITED STATES EDITION

06 November 2026 00:44 EST

ORIGINAL ANNOUNCEMENT [Text]

Combines Aimpoint Digitals forward-deployed AI engineering and platform expertise with EXLs deep industry knowledge, proprietary agentic AI capabilities, and operational scale to transform mission-critical enterprise workflowsDeepens expertise across leading AI platforms including Anthropic, Databricks, and Snowflake, accelerating the journey from AI strategy to production and measurable business outcomesAdvances EXLs strategy to deliver end-to-end enterprise AI transformation, connecting AI development, engineering, deployment, evaluation, and managed operations

NEW YORK, Nov. 05, 2026 -- EXL [NASDAQ: EXLS], a global data and AI company, today announced it has acquired Aimpoint Digital, a data and AI consulting and platform engineering firm known for building enterprise data and AI platforms with deep expertise in market-leading partners including Anthropic, Databricks, and Snowflake. The acquisition strengthens EXLs ability to support clients across the full AI transformation lifecycle and deepens EXLs existing relationships with key technology partners. Financial terms of the transaction were not disclosed.

Enterprises are investing heavily in AI, yet many organizations continue to struggle to move AI initiatives from pilot to production-scale value. Aimpoint Digital has built a reputation for moving client workloads onto leading data and AI platforms with speed. By bringing Aimpoint Digital into EXL, the two companies will scale that strength across more workflows and functions, extending AIs business impact across the enterprise and broadening EXLs offering for enterprise-wide transformation.

Clients require a partner who can take them from AI ambition to impact at enterprise scale, said Rohit Kapoor, chairman and chief executive officer, EXL. Aimpoint Digitals forward-deployed strategists and engineers bring the hands-on expertise to turn AI strategy into production reality on the platforms our clients trust most. By bringing their advisory and engineering talent together with EXLs data foundations, industry expertise, agentic AI solutions, and operational scale, we can reimagine workflows and give clients a single, accountable partner for the entire journey from strategy and architecture through deployment and managed AI operations.

Joining EXL lets us do more breakthrough work on a much larger scale, said Ben Yopp, chief executive officer and cofounder, Aimpoint Digital. We bring deep AI strategy and engineering talent and EXL brings industry expertise, global reach and the ability to run AI in operations. Together, we can take clients most important AI initiatives into production and keep them performing at the highest levels.

EXL and Aimpoint Digital will provide clients with greater continuity from the first strategy conversation through architecture, engineering, production deployment, and managed operations. This connected approach is designed to reduce handoffs, establish clearer accountability, and accelerate the path to measurable outcomes.

In a marketplace where demand for strategic data and AI engagements outpaces available talent, the combination adds approximately 250 experienced AI engineers, platform architects, analytics consultants, decision scientists, and AI strategists to EXL's data and AI capabilities. This expands EXLs forward-deployed talent base, puts senior AI expertise closer to client teams, and adds capacity for complex, multi-year transformations.

Aimpoint Digital also brings deep expertise working with strategic partners, expanding EXL's partner ecosystem footprint and deepening expertise on partners such as Anthropic, Databricks, and Snowflake. This will help clients reimagine core workflows while building on the platforms they trust and helping them get to production sooner.

The acquisition builds on EXLs recent acquisition of iMerit, a global leader in AI model training, evaluation and reinforcement learning and reinforcement learning. Together, iMerit and Aimpoint Digital extend EXLs ability to support clients from strategy and architecture through engineering, deployment, and managed AI operations reflecting EXLs disciplined strategy of extending its data and AI capabilities through investments in innovation, acquisitions, and talent.

About EXL

EXL (NASDAQ: EXLS) is a global data and AI company that offers services and solutions to reinvent client business models, drive better outcomes and unlock growth with speed. EXL propels enterprises to go beyond AI ambition to impact by combining the power of data, AI, and deep industry context with trusted execution. Our clients include the world's leading corporations across insurance, healthcare, banking and capital markets, retail, communications and media, and energy and infrastructure, among others. EXL was founded in 1999 with the core values of innovation, collaboration, excellence, integrity and respect. We are headquartered in New York and have over 74,000 employees spanning six continents. For more information, visit https://www.exlservice.com.

About Aimpoint Digital

Aimpoint Digital is an AI and data consulting firm that turns AI ambition into production reality, built on a scalable foundation of data and analytics. The company architects, builds, and operationalizes enterprise AI and modern data solutions, helping organizations transform fragmented environments into scalable platforms, intelligent applications, and measurable business outcomes.

Nov 04, 2026: Boston Scientific Partners with Blackstone on Innovative $455M Heart Pump Initiative

Boston Scientific Corp has announced a collaboration with Blackstone Life Sciences to co-fund the VITALYST heart pump program, a significant development in the realm of cardiac care. The deal, valued at $455 million, marks the first partnership of its kind between these two organizations, set to support the advancement of a temporary percutaneous transvalvular circulatory support system. This initiative is particularly relevant on 4th November 2026, as it aims to address urgent medical needs faced by patients suffering from acute myocardial infarction cardiogenic shock (AMI-CS).

The VITALYST system is designed to restore essential blood circulation for patients experiencing AMI-CS, a condition characterized by the inability of the heart to deliver adequate oxygenated blood to vital organs. Despite advancements in cardiac care, AMI-CS remains a critical challenge, with high mortality rates. The VITALYST heart pump seeks to bridge the gaps in treatment by enhancing clinical performance, improving patient safety, and providing practical usability for healthcare professionals involved in interventional procedures.

Notably, the VITALYST pump employs a microaxial flow architecture capable of delivering blood at rates exceeding four liters per minute, while also alleviating pressure on the left ventricle. This innovative design preserves blood supply to organs during high-risk procedures and incorporates a magnetic force-powered impeller, which reduces the risk of blood vessel damage. Additionally, Boston Scientific has optimized the motor and catheter assembly to secure the device more effectively and minimize the destruction of red blood cells during use.

Nicholas Galakatos, global head of Blackstone Life Sciences, expressed confidence in Boston Scientific's capabilities, stating, "Boston Scientific has built a leading cardiovascular franchise, with deep expertise in developing and commercializing transformative technologies for patients and physicians." He emphasized the potential of the VITALYST system to enhance the safety and efficacy of existing mechanical circulatory support technologies, underscoring the importance of their collaboration in bringing innovative medical devices to market.

Furthermore, Boston Scientific recently disclosed positive six-month primary endpoint data from its SIMPLAAFY randomized controlled trial, which examined its WATCHMAN FLX Pro left atrial appendage closure device. Conducted among patients with non-valvular atrial fibrillation (NVAF), the trial evaluated the effectiveness of single-drug approaches compared to dual antiplatelet therapy following device placement. The WATCHMAN FLX Pro is engineered to mitigate stroke risk linked to clot formation in NVAF patients, showcasing Boston Scientific's ongoing commitment to advancing cardiac solutions.

The firm’s historical performance and strategic foresight continue to attract significant capital commitments.

Nov 04, 2026: DIP Holding Attracts Global Interest for $529 Million Dubai IPO Amid Blackstone Backing

Dubai Investments Park Holding Plc (DIP Holding) has successfully raised $529 million through its initial public offering (IPO) in Dubai, demonstrating strong international demand that notably included participation from Saudi Arabia's Public Investment Fund (PIF) and a unit of Blackstone Inc., according to sources familiar with the matter. The offering, which is scheduled to begin trading on November 11, 2026, was priced at the midpoint of the indicative range, a departure from the typical pricing patterns seen in UAE listings.

The DIP Holding IPO saw Dubai Investments PJSC sell 20% of the company at 1.12 dirhams per share, positioning the overall valuation at approximately 9.7 billion dirhams. The company described the offering as significantly oversubscribed, although specific coverage figures have not been disclosed. The involvement of major players like PIF, which manages assets totaling around $905 billion, signals a renewed interest in the region, particularly as relations between Riyadh and Abu Dhabi appear to be improving.

This IPO comes at a challenging time for the market, as equities across the Gulf region have faced declines since the onset of geopolitical tensions earlier in the year. The Dubai Financial Market's main index has reported a drop of around 7% in that timeframe. Despite this backdrop, the robust interest in DIP Holding’s offering suggests that quality listings can still attract investment even during uncertain market conditions.

DIP Holding oversees Dubai Investments Park, a significant mixed-use development spanning 2,300 hectares that has been operational since 1997. This site houses various facilities, including warehouses, hotels, and educational institutions. The successful pricing of the IPO amid a soft market highlights the potential for growth in quality assets, even as broader regional indices experience volatility. Investors are keen to understand how such strategic moves may affect their portfolios, especially in a fluctuating economic landscape.

The upcoming trading debut of DIP Holding will be closely watched as it may set the tone for future listings in the region. With Blackstone's involvement, alongside the PIF, the transaction exemplifies a notable trend of foreign investment interest in the UAE's real estate and infrastructure sectors. Market analysts will be monitoring the performance of DIP Holding post-listing to gauge investor sentiment and the potential for further capital inflows into similar ventures.

Nov 02, 2026: Blackstone Inc. Takes Strategic Leap with $3.1bn LNG Canada Investment

Blackstone Inc. is making a significant move in the energy sector by leading a $3.1 billion investment in LNG Canada, acquiring a 49.9% stake from Mitsubishi Corporation. The announcement, made on 2nd November 2026, positions Blackstone as a major player in one of North America's most pivotal liquefied natural gas projects.

Reports indicate that while Blackstone's official investment figure stands at $3.1 billion, the immediate stake purchase from Mitsubishi is valued closer to $1.6 billion. This discrepancy suggests that the larger figure includes future capital commitments related to LNG Canada’s planned Phase 2 expansion, which aims to double the terminal’s export capacity to 28 million tonnes per year. Mitsubishi will retain its full LNG offtake and remain the controlling partner in the project, allowing Blackstone's consortium—comprising CPP Investments, OMERS, IMCO, and PSP Investments—to gain financial exposure without operational control.

LNG Canada is recognized as the country's first large-scale liquefied natural gas export terminal, an aspect highlighted in Blackstone's announcement as crucial to the asset's attractiveness. CPP Investments' Geoff Souter emphasized LNG Canada as 'a high-quality, globally strategic asset with strong long-term fundamentals and attractive return potential.' The deal aligns with Blackstone's strategy of collaborating with Canadian pension funds on significant domestic infrastructure investments, notably a CDN$7 billion equity investment in Rogers Communications completed last year.

Blackstone's financial capabilities are underscored by its recent performance, with the firm reporting a net income of $1.23 billion for the quarter ending 30th June 2026. This figure reflects a robust increase compared to net income ranging between $444 million and $847 million throughout 2024 and 2025. The firm’s shares are currently trading at $119.10, experiencing a modest rise of 0.61% on the day, and a notable increase of 3.88% over the past 20 trading sessions. The timing of this investment also benefits from a decline in US Treasury yields, making financing large infrastructure projects more affordable.

Looking ahead, investors and analysts are keenly awaiting further disclosures regarding the transaction's full terms, particularly how much of the $3.1 billion figure represents the cash amount paid to Mitsubishi versus the capital allocated for LNG Canada’s planned expansion. As Blackstone Inc. continues to navigate the complexities of the energy market, its strategic decisions will be closely monitored by stakeholders eager to assess the long-term viability of this investment.

Oct 30, 2026: Blackstone Expands Indian Operations with New Base in Bengaluru

Blackstone has officially opened a new Global Capability Center in Bengaluru, marking its second operational base in India. This strategic move comes as part of the alternative asset manager's ongoing commitment to enhance support for its worldwide funds. The facility will primarily focus on operational execution and investment-support services while the front-office teams will continue to operate from the firm's existing Mumbai headquarters.

The Bengaluru center, established on 30th October 2026, will be led by Susan Yung, a senior managing director in Blackstone Credit & Insurance’s investment operations division. Ms. Yung brings over 25 years of financial sector experience and has previously worked in Bengaluru during her tenure at Goldman Sachs. This new location will house in-house staff and professional services partnerships aimed at improving the overall efficiency of Blackstone’s global operations.

By setting up operations in Bengaluru, Blackstone aligns itself with a growing trend among institutional investors to establish in-house execution platforms for functions like fund accounting, data compliance, and risk analytics. This strategy not only helps in securing captive tenancy within proprietary business parks—such as the jointly-owned Prestige Lakeshore Drive—but also reduces reliance on external business process outsourcing, thereby cutting operational costs.

Over the past two decades, Blackstone has cultivated a significant presence in the Indian market, emerging as the largest owner of office, retail, and logistics real estate in the country. The firm has successfully taken 14 Indian portfolio companies public and played a crucial role in launching five of the six listed real estate investment trusts (REITs) in India. This expansion into Bengaluru underscores Blackstone's long-term commitment to the region and its ambition to deepen its engagement with Indian capital markets.

As the company moves forward, market observers will closely monitor hiring trends at the new Bengaluru center and any potential transfer of further investment-support functions from Blackstone's offices in Europe and North America. Additionally, upcoming regulatory filings related to new Indian REIT listings and asset roll-ups in the retail and warehousing sectors will be pivotal in shaping Blackstone's future strategies in the region.

Oct 28, 2026: Blackstone Inc. Launches $1.1 Billion Senior Notes Offering

NEW YORK - Blackstone Inc. (NYSE:BX) priced an offering of $1.1 billion in senior notes through its indirect subsidiary Blackstone Reg Finance Co. L.L.C., according to a press release statement. The transaction was disclosed on 28th October 2026, highlighting the firm's ongoing strategy to utilize debt financing to bolster its capital structure.

The offering consists of $600 million of 5.650% senior notes due 2031 and $500 million of 5.900% senior notes due 2033. These senior notes will be guaranteed by Blackstone Inc. along with five of its indirect subsidiaries: Blackstone Holdings I L.P., Blackstone Holdings AI L.P., Blackstone Holdings II L.P., Blackstone Holdings III L.P., and Blackstone Holdings IV L.P. This guarantees the debt, providing investors with an added layer of security.

Blackstone stated that it intends to use the proceeds from this notes offering for general corporate purposes, a move that is expected to enhance its operational flexibility. The company has been proactive in managing its financing needs, and this latest offering is in line with its strategic objectives to maintain liquidity and support growth initiatives.

The notes were offered through an effective shelf registration statement filed with the U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission. The underwriting team for this offering includes notable financial institutions such as Citigroup Global Markets Inc., Goldman Sachs & Co. LLC, Morgan Stanley & Co. LLC, SMBC Nikko Securities America, Inc., and Wells Fargo Securities, LLC, underscoring the confidence in Blackstone's financial health.

As Blackstone Inc. continues to navigate the complexities of the financial markets, this offering reflects its commitment to maintaining a robust capital base while strategically positioning itself for future opportunities. Investors are likely to keep a close watch on how these funds will be deployed in the coming months.

Oct 27, 2026: Blackstone Leads $63B AI Compute Financing with Major Banking Partners

In a landmark transaction aimed at bolstering artificial intelligence capabilities, Bank of America, Blackstone, Citi, and Morgan Stanley have come together to arrange a substantial $63 billion financing package. This funding is specifically designed to support the deployment of AI compute capacity leveraging Broadcom’s cutting-edge technology. The announcement was made on 28th October 2026, with the financing expected to play a pivotal role in facilitating Anthropic's advanced AI initiatives.

The financing will be executed through a specially formed special-purpose vehicle (SPV), which has been established to manage the acquisition of Broadcom's XPU AI accelerators and networking solutions. The newly formed SPV will lease these technologies to Anthropic, marking a significant step in the company's journey towards enhancing its AI capabilities. Deliveries of the technology are anticipated to commence in calendar 2027, aligning with Anthropic's strategic goals.

Bank of America, Citi, and Morgan Stanley are taking the lead in arranging the first-lien portion of the financing, while Blackstone is stepping in as the sole provider of the second-lien financing. This collaborative effort underscores the financial community's confidence in the potential of AI technologies and the long-term vision shared by the involved parties. Blackstone also plays a crucial role by providing residual value support to the SPV, equivalent to the amount of the first-lien financing.

Jon Gray, president and COO of Blackstone, commented on the transaction, emphasizing the rapid evolution of AI technologies and the necessity for robust compute capacity to meet growing demands. SECTION 6 BLACKSTONE INC 10-Q: CONSEQUENTIAL MANAGEMENT DECISIONS - QUARTER ENDING SEP 30, 2026

Capital Returns: $24.0 Million of Repurchases and a $1.29 Per Share Dividend; $1.6 Billion of Authorization Remaining

During the three and six months ended June 30, 2026, Blackstone repurchased 0.2 million and 0.4 million shares of common stock, pursuant to its repurchase program, at a total cost of $24.0 million and $48.4 million, respectively, under the program authorized by the board of directors on July 16, 2024 for up to $2.0 billion of common stock and Blackstone Holdings Partnership Units; $1.6 billion remained available for repurchases as of June 30, 2026. The program does not have a specified expiration date and may be changed, suspended or discontinued at any time, with repurchases made in open market transactions, in privately negotiated transactions or otherwise. With respect to the second quarter of fiscal year 2026, the Company paid a dividend of $1.29 per share, aggregating to $2.45 per share of common stock for the two fiscal quarters ended June 30, 2026, compared to aggregate dividends of $4.74 per share with respect to fiscal year 2025. Management's stated intention is to pay a quarterly dividend representing approximately 85% of Blackstone Inc.'s share of Distributable Earnings, subject to adjustment by the board of directors, and the declaration and payment of dividends remain at the sole discretion of the board, which may change the dividend policy at any time, including reducing or eliminating dividends. Repurchases and dividends are among the stated uses of liquidity identified by management, alongside funding general partner and co-investment commitments, business expansion, operating expenses, income taxes and distributions to holders of Blackstone Holdings Partnership Units.

Capital Markets Advisory Revenues Move to Credit & Insurance; Segment Transaction, Advisory and Other Fees Rose 556%

Beginning in the three months ended June 30, 2026, capital markets advisory revenues generated in connection with certain financing transactions in infrastructure and asset based credit strategies are presented in the Credit & Insurance segment; previously, all capital markets advisory revenues were included in the Private Equity segment as part of the BXCM business. The filing attributes the Credit & Insurance segment's Transaction, Advisory and Other Fees, Net increase of $77.8 million, to $91,769 thousand from $13,980 thousand in the prior-year quarter, primarily to capital markets advisory revenues, with the change representing an increase of 556%. For the six months ended June 30, 2026, the same line increased $72.9 million, primarily attributable to capital markets advisory revenues in infrastructure and asset based credit. The reclassification shifts a stream of transaction-driven fee revenue out of the Private Equity segment's reported results, which will affect how the two segments' revenue growth and fee-related earnings are read relative to prior periods. Investors comparing segment trends should note the disclosure of the presentation change alongside the current-period results.

Liquidity: $800.0 Million Drawn on the $4.325 Billion Revolving Credit Facility, With $2.5 Billion of Cash on Hand

Blackstone drew on its $4.325 billion unsecured Revolving Credit Facility with Citibank, N.A. as administrative agent during the quarter ended March 31, 2026, with the increase in Loans Payable of $752.1 million primarily attributable to the draw; as of June 30, 2026, $800.0 million of borrowings were outstanding under the facility, which has a maturity date of October 16, 2030. The draw sits within a balance sheet that also included $2.5 billion in Cash and Cash Equivalents, $401.5 million invested in Corporate Treasury Investments and $8.5 billion in Other Investments, which included $7.8 billion of liquid investments, against $13.2 billion in borrowings that included the bond issuances and the $800.0 million of Revolving Credit Facility borrowings. The facility's financial covenants consist of a maximum net leverage ratio and a requirement to keep a minimum amount of fee-earning assets under management, each tested quarterly. Management identifies the facility as one of multiple liquidity sources, including annual cash flows, accumulated earnings, proceeds from senior note issuances and liquid investments, and states that the sources of liquidity described are more than sufficient to fund working capital requirements. With the committed facility, the drawn balance and the disclosed cash and liquid investment holdings, the liquidity position is consistent with that characterization of available resources.

IRS Issues CAMT Guidance on February 18, 2026; Blackstone Expects No Material Impact on Provision for Taxes

On February 18, 2026, the U.S. Internal Revenue Service issued guidance which provides for additional adjustments to the calculation of the corporate alternative minimum tax (CAMT). Based on the available guidance, Blackstone does not believe such adjustments to the CAMT calculation will materially impact its Provision for Taxes. The Company's provision for income taxes is comprised of current and deferred taxes, and management notes that significant judgment is required in estimating the provision, current and deferred tax balances, valuation allowances and uncertain tax positions. With an effective tax rate of 16.1% for the three months ended June 30, 2026, and Taxes and Related Payables of $209,833 thousand included in the Distributable Earnings reconciliation, the CAMT conclusion is one of several tax-position items that bear on earnings available for distribution. The filing also cautions that revisions in estimates and/or actual costs of a tax assessment may ultimately be materially different from the recorded accruals and unrecognized tax benefits.

Total Revenues Increased 36% to $5,043,978 Thousand; Net Income Attributable to Blackstone Inc. Up 61%

Total Revenues were $5,043,978 thousand for the three months ended June 30, 2026, an increase of $1,332,078 thousand, or 36%, compared to $3,711,900 thousand in the prior-year quarter, with the increase primarily attributable to increases of $867.1 million in Investment Income, $237.0 million in Other Revenue and $230.5 million in Management and Advisory Fees, Net. Income Before Provision for Taxes was $2,808,452 thousand, an increase of $892,825 thousand, or 47%, from $1,915,627 thousand, and Net Income Attributable to Blackstone Inc. was $1,229,189 thousand, an increase of $464,945 thousand, or 61%, from $764,244 thousand. The Provision for Taxes was $452,386 thousand, resulting in an effective tax rate of 16.1% compared to 15.1% in the prior-year quarter; the increase in the effective rate related primarily to the impact of Non-Controlling Interests in Consolidated Entities. Total Expenses were $2,375,612 thousand, an increase of $443,009 thousand, or 23%, from $1,932,603 thousand, primarily attributable to an increase of $392.6 million in Total Compensation and Benefits, of which $317.6 million was an increase in Performance Allocations Compensation. For the six months ended June 30, 2026, Total Revenues increased 24% to $8,661,573 thousand, and Net Income Attributable to Blackstone Inc. was $1,878,918 thousand, an increase of $499,822 thousand, or 36%, from $1,379,096 thousand.

Fee Related Earnings Were $1,783,428 Thousand for the Quarter, With Increases in All Four Segments

Fee Related Earnings, which Blackstone presents as insight into the profitability of the portion of the business that is not dependent on realization activity, were $1,783,428 thousand for the three months ended June 30, 2026, compared to $1,459,503 thousand for the prior-year quarter; for the six months ended June 30, 2026, Fee Related Earnings were $3,331,409 thousand versus $2,721,559 thousand. Each of the four segments reported higher Fee Related Earnings: Private Equity increased $197.5 million to $716.9 million, Real Estate increased $69.4 million to $613.0 million, Credit & Insurance increased $37.0 million to $370.1 million, and Multi-Asset Investing increased $20.0 million to $83.4 million. The Private Equity increase was primarily attributable to increases of $199.1 million in Fee Related Performance Revenues and $88.3 million in Management and Advisory Fees, Net, partially offset by an increase of $70.6 million in Fee Related Compensation, and the Real Estate increase reflected a $153.1 million increase in Fee Related Performance Revenues attributable to higher Fee Related Performance Revenues in BREIT. Distributable Earnings, which management uses to assess performance and amounts available for dividends to stockholders, were $1,977,249 thousand for the three months ended June 30, 2026, compared to $1,565,763 thousand, with Total Segment Distributable Earnings of $2,197,688 thousand versus $1,785,421 thousand. Adjusted EBITDA, derived from Distributable Earnings, was $2,357,684 thousand compared to $1,912,453 thousand for the prior-year quarter.

Private Equity Distributable Earnings Increased 31% to $981.5 Million on Higher Fee Related Earnings and Net Realizations

Private Equity segment Distributable Earnings were $981.5 million for the three months ended June 30, 2026, an increase of $230.1 million, or 31%, compared to $751.4 million in the prior-year quarter, attributable to increases of $197.5 million in Fee Related Earnings and of $32.5 million in Net Realizations. Fee Related Earnings of $716.9 million increased 38%, with Fee Related Performance Revenues of $391.4 million up $199.1 million, or 103%, primarily attributable to higher performance revenues in BXPE and crystallization of performance revenues in BXINFRA. Base Management Fees increased 13% to $681,444 thousand. Management reported strong performance across strategies in the second quarter of 2026, led by the performance of digital infrastructure investments and the energy portfolio, while noting that the ongoing conflict in the Middle East has had the effect of slowing overall realization activity in the near term and that the Company has continued to take advantage of an initial public offerings market that has strengthened considerably. For the six months ended June 30, 2026, segment Distributable Earnings were $2.0 billion, an increase of $651.1 million, or 49%, compared to $1.3 billion.

Real Estate Distributable Earnings Increased 32% to $745.3 Million; BREIT Fee Related Performance Revenues Increased 171%

Real Estate segment Distributable Earnings were $745.3 million for the three months ended June 30, 2026, an increase of $179.4 million, or 32%, compared to $565.9 million in the prior-year quarter, attributable to increases of $110.0 million in Net Realizations and of $69.4 million in Fee Related Earnings. Fee Related Earnings of $613.0 million increased 13%, with Fee Related Performance Revenues of $242.7 million up $153.1 million, or 171%, attributable to higher Fee Related Performance Revenues in BREIT. Realized Performance Revenues were $210.9 million, an increase of $167.3 million, or 384%, primarily attributable to higher Realized Performance Revenues in BREP. Base Management Fees declined 6% to $630,097 thousand, and segment Total Assets Under Management decreased $1.1 billion during the quarter to $314.1 billion, with realizations of $6.6 billion partially offset by inflows of $8.2 billion. Management stated that overall values in the segment appreciated modestly in the second quarter of 2026, led by strength in digital infrastructure and partly offset by declines in life science office and certain other areas, with debt capital markets continuing to exhibit strength in the quarter.

Credit & Insurance Distributable Earnings Declined 6% to $373.2 Million; BCRED Redemption Requests Remain Elevated

Credit & Insurance segment Distributable Earnings were $373.2 million for the three months ended June 30, 2026, a decrease of $22.5 million, or -6%, compared to $395.8 million in the prior-year quarter, attributable to a decrease of $59.6 million in Net Realizations, partially offset by an increase of $37.0 million in Fee Related Earnings. Fee Related Earnings were $370.1 million, an increase of $37.0 million, or 11%, and Transaction, Advisory and Other Fees, Net increased $77.8 million, or 556%, to $91,769 thousand, primarily attributable to capital markets advisory revenues. Management disclosed that redemption requests in BCRED remain elevated due, in large part, to heightened press and market attention around private credit, as well as concerns about decelerating performance, and that subscriptions to BCRED, albeit solid, decelerated from the prior quarter, resulting in net outflows in the second quarter of 2026. Management also stated that so far in the third quarter, redemption requests are significantly lower relative to the same time in the second quarter. While defaults in direct lending have risen and are expected to continue to rise from a historically low level, the segment's holdings are predominantly in senior secured credit with significant equity subordination from institutional borrowers, and management states it believes this should position the segment well. For the six months ended June 30, 2026, segment Distributable Earnings were $746.3 million, a decrease of $152.8 million, or -17%, compared to $899.1 million.

Multi-Asset Investing Distributable Earnings Increased 35% to $97.7 Million; Absolute Return Posts 25th Consecutive Positive Quarter

Multi-Asset Investing segment Distributable Earnings were $97.7 million for the three months ended June 30, 2026, an increase of $25.3 million, or 35%, compared to $72.3 million in the prior-year quarter, attributable to increases of $20.0 million in Fee Related Earnings and of $5.4 million in Net Realizations. All strategies in the segment exhibited positive performance in the second quarter of 2026, driven by strong performance of investments in the artificial-intelligence sector, and the Absolute Return Composite posted its twenty-fifth consecutive quarter of positive performance. The filing states that continued strong performance in the segment has and is contributing to favorable fundraising dynamics, including the segment's best single month of fundraising with $4.8 billion of inflows subsequent to quarter end. Fee Related Earnings were $83.4 million, an increase of $20.0 million, compared to $63.4 million in the prior-year quarter, with Management and Advisory Fees, Net of $155.9 million up $24.1 million on higher Base Management Fees. For the six months ended June 30, 2026, segment Distributable Earnings were $177.9 million, an increase of $49.9 million, or 39%, compared to $128.0 million.

Total Assets Under Management Reached $1,346.3 Billion; Fee-Earning AUM $961.6 Billion and Perpetual Capital $555.6 Billion

Total Assets Under Management were $1,346.3 billion at June 30, 2026, an increase of $42.2 billion compared to $1,304.0 billion at March 31, 2026, and an increase of $71.3 billion compared to $1,274.9 billion at December 31, 2025. Within the quarterly increase, Private Equity Total Assets Under Management rose $24.2 billion to $454.2 billion, Credit & Insurance rose $11.9 billion to $469.3 billion, Multi-Asset Investing rose $7.3 billion to $108.6 billion, and Real Estate declined $1.1 billion to $314.1 billion. Fee-Earning Assets Under Management were $961.6 billion at June 30, 2026, an increase of $24.0 billion from $937.6 billion at March 31, 2026, and an increase of $39.9 billion from $921.7 billion at December 31, 2025. Perpetual Capital Total Assets Under Management were $555.6 billion, an increase of $15.9 billion from $539.7 billion at March 31, 2026, and an increase of $31.9 billion from $523.6 billion at December 31, 2025. Perpetual Capital represents capital with an indefinite term that is not in liquidation, which management identifies as having the ability to generate recurring revenues in a different manner than traditional fund structures.

Net Accrued Performance Revenues Increased to $7,468 Million on $4.6 Billion of Net Performance Revenues

Net Accrued Performance Revenues, which Blackstone describes as an indicator of potential future realized performance revenues based on the current investment portfolio of the funds and vehicles it manages, were $7,468 million at June 30, 2026, compared to $6,608 million at June 30, 2025. For the twelve months ended June 30, 2026, the receivable increased due to Net Performance Revenues of $4.6 billion, partially offset by net realized distributions of $3.8 billion. By segment, Private Equity Net Accrued Performance Revenues were $6,081 million versus $5,212 million, Real Estate was $891 million versus $923 million, Credit & Insurance was $252 million versus $369 million, and Multi-Asset Investing was $243 million versus $103 million. The measure is defined as accrued performance revenues receivable, net of related accrued performance compensation payable, excluding performance revenues realized but not yet distributed and clawback amounts. Realized performance revenues are a component of segment Distributable Earnings, and Distributable Earnings is the measure management uses to assess performance and amounts available for dividends to stockholders, which links the level of accrued performance revenues to the capital-return framework.

SECTION 11 CORPORATE PROFILE

11.1 Activities

Blackstone Inc., a global leader in alternative asset management and listed on the New York Stock Exchange, specializes in private equity, real estate, credit, and hedge fund investments. Founded in 1985, the firm manages a diverse portfolio of assets for institutional and individual investors, focusing on sourcing, executing, and overseeing investments that deliver long-term value across sectors like infrastructure and growth equity. Its core activities include raising capital, making strategic investments, and providing advisory services to enhance portfolio performance. Among its fastest-growing segments are private credit and real estate, fueled by rising demand for alternative financing solutions and urbanization trends, respectively. This expansion has positioned Blackstone as a key player in global markets, with a strong track record of innovation and operational excellence. It is the 3rd largest Asset manager company by market capitalization among NYSE- and TXSE-listed stocks.

### 5. [TaskUs Q3 Earnings Call Highlights](https://ct.moreover.com/?a=60795697892&p=21l&v=1&x=1uEX5AKOdCxhz_JRBmCLkA)
- **Outlet(s):** Transcript Daily | **Published:** `2026-11-07` | **Sentiment:** `NEUTRAL` | **Positioning Relevance Score:** `1.00`
- **Matched Innodata Pillars:** Robotic Engineering, Agent Engineering, Agent Reliability Engineering (ARE)
- **Matched Competitors:** Appen, TELUS Digital, Sama, TaskUs, CloudFactory, iMerit
- **Coverage Snippet:** November 6th, 2026 ( Transcript Daily - Delivered by Newstex )

TaskUs (NASDAQ:TASK) reported record third-quarter revenue and raised its full-year outlook, as growth in artificial intelligence services and customer experience work offset a decline in business from its largest client.

Revenue rose 5.4% from a year earlier to $314.7 million, exceeding the top end of the companys guidance by $12.7 million. Adjusted earnings before interest, taxes, depreciation and amortization were $59.1 million, or 18.8% of revenue, about 10 basis points above the companys margin guidance.

Adjusted net income fell to $31.3 million, or 34 cents per share, from $39 million, or 42 cents per share, a year earlier. Chief Financial Officer Rishabh Khemka attributed the decline primarily to higher interest expense following a refinancing and higher service costs, partly offset by lower income tax expense.

AI Services Growth Offsets Largest-Client Decline

AI Services revenue rose 27.7% to $75 million, marking its eighth consecutive quarter of growth above 25%. It became TaskUs second-largest service line during the quarter, driven primarily by work in autonomous vehicles, autonomous delivery and robotics.

Co-founder and Chief Executive Officer Bryce Maddock said revenue from autonomous vehicles and robotics is expected to exceed $100 million for full-year 2026, more than three times the 2025 level. He expects AI Services to remain the companys fastest-growing service line in 2027.

Digital Customer Experience revenue increased 7.1% to $175.9 million. Trust & Safety revenue, which includes content moderation and financial crime and compliance services, fell 15.8% to $63.9 million, primarily because of lower revenue from the largest client.

That client accounted for 17% of third-quarter revenue, down from 27% a year earlier, as its automation and cost-optimization initiatives reduced work for TaskUs. Excluding the client, revenue grew nearly 20%, while revenue from the companys second- through 20th-largest clients increased 37%, Maddock said.

Maddock expects continued pressure from the largest client for the next few quarters. He said most of its automation work has occurred, although further, more modest automation is expected in 2027. TaskUs anticipates benefiting from the clients vendor consolidation and has won work supporting its personal agentic AI initiatives.

The CEO said year-over-year growth from the client is not expected in the first couple of quarters of 2027, but is possible in the second half.

Higher Outlook and Investment Plans

TaskUs raised its full-year 2026 revenue outlook to between $1.249 billion and $1.251 billion. At the $1.25 billion midpoint, the forecast represents 5.6% growth from 2025. The company maintained its expectation for an adjusted EBITDA margin of approximately 19%.

Management also increased its full-year adjusted free cash flow forecast to $115 million to $125 million, a $5 million increase at the midpoint. For the fourth quarter, TaskUs expects revenue of $319 million to $321 million and an adjusted EBITDA margin of approximately 19.5%.

Maddock said the company plans to increase investment in physical AI, autonomous vehicles and the AI transformation of its customer experience business in 2027. He cautioned that the spending will constrain near-term margin expansion, though management expects it to support longer-term growth.

Khemka said growth in AI Services is currently weighted toward lower-margin onshore work. Over the medium to long term, he expects more of that work to move offshore. In the meantime, TaskUs is using AI tools, overhead controls and pricing discussions with customers to help offset margin pressure.

New AI Work and Cash Flow

Maddock said TaskUs secured a contract to provide remote teleoperation support for a technology companys last-mile delivery robots and expanded work with a consumer robotics developer. The company is also developing datasets using short-term rental properties as testing environments.

In AI safety, TaskUs is working toward a partnership with a large language model developer on solutions intended to monitor, detect and help mitigate suicide and self-harm incidents, Maddock said. He also described work in adversarial testing and red teaming for model developers and other clients.

Cash and cash equivalents stood at $189.7 million at Sept. 30, compared with $211.7 million at the end of 2025. Cash generated from operations through the first nine months rose to $120.1 million from $107.5 million a year earlier. Khemka said the company expects full-year capital expenditures of approximately $45 million, $15 million below its initial outlook.

About TaskUs (NASDAQ:TASK)

TaskUs, Inc (NASDAQ: TASK) is a provider of digital outsourcing and customer experience services for technology companies and other high-growth businesses. The company helps clients manage customer interactions, back-office operations and other business processes through a combination of trained personnel, technology and data-driven workflows.

Its services include customer care, technical support, content moderation, trust and safety, fraud prevention, artificial intelligence operations and back-office support.

SECTION 2 RECENT NEWS

Recent News 1 of 20, Source: HourlyBusinessNews

Oct 29, 2026: Li Auto Unveils 2026 i6: New Battery and Chip Technology at Competitive Price

Li Auto Inc. has officially launched its 2026 Li i6, priced at a nationwide unified retail price of 249,800 yuan (approximately $37,000), with a limited-time launch price of 239,800 yuan (approximately $36,000). This event took place on the evening of 28th October 2026-10-30 12:59:53.281114, marking the first annual refresh of the five-seat all-electric SUV a year after its debut. The most significant change is the incorporation of the self-developed Lianshan 5C battery, the M100 chip, and the new-generation cockpit platform as standard across all trims, along with upgrades to range, chassis, and comfort features.

Li Xiang, chairman and CEO of Li Auto, emphasized that a successful product stems from genuine market feedback. He stated that the 2026 i6 is the result of understanding user needs, aiming to position it as Li Auto's highest-selling vehicle globally. The i6 has quickly become the most popular model in Li Auto's lineup, with cumulative deliveries nearing 200,000 units and contributing substantially to the company's overall sales. In the first three quarters of 2026, Li Auto delivered a total of 293,400 vehicles, with the i6 alone accounting for about 160,000 units.

The electric powertrain is at the core of this refresh. The 2026 Li i6 features the upgraded Lianshan 5C battery, with a capacity of 96 kWh. The CLTC range for the rear-wheel-drive version has increased from 720 km to 780 km, while the all-wheel-drive version's range has improved from 660 km to 710 km. Li Auto reported that its supercharging stations have seen over 2.8 million uses in the past year, indicating strong customer engagement with the i6's capabilities.

On the technology front, the new model is equipped with the self-developed M100 chip and the latest VLA 2.5 driver-assist platform, which enhances the vehicle's perception of road environments. The cockpit features a Qualcomm Snapdragon 8797 chip and a 29-inch 6K panoramic screen, with an optional 21-inch 4K entertainment screen for rear passengers. Safety and comfort have also been prioritized, with advanced suspension systems and upgraded seating configurations aimed at enhancing the driving experience.

Despite the promising launch of the i6, Li Auto has faced challenges in overall growth, with a decline in vehicle deliveries and revenue in 2025 and the first half of 2026. However, the company is optimistic about the i6's potential both domestically and in overseas markets, particularly with plans to launch the European version in early 2027. Li Auto aims to leverage its self-developed technologies and the i6's features to establish a stronger foothold in the global electric vehicle market.

Li Auto (NASDAQ: LI, HKEX: 2015) has announced a significant pledge to its customers, promising a new vehicle of the same configuration if its in-house Lianshan battery self-ignites due to a quality defect, necessitating the scrapping of the car. Oct 16, 2026: Li Auto Inc. Secures RMB1.625 Billion for Chip Business Expansion

Li Auto Inc. ('Li Auto' or the 'Company') (Nasdaq: LI; HKEX: 2015), a leader in China's new energy vehicle market, has announced a significant equity financing initiative aimed at bolstering its chip and domain controller business. The announcement, made on the 16th of October 2026, details that Shanghai Mahe Xin Technology Co., Ltd. ('MHX Technology'), a wholly-owned subsidiary of Li Auto, has entered into subscription agreements for equity financing totaling RMB1.625 billion.

Under this financing arrangement, Li Auto, through a wholly-owned subsidiary, and external investors, including Hangzhou Jingtao, Lanchi Ventures, Huaye Tiancheng, and MiraclePlus, will contribute RMB1.0 billion and RMB625 million respectively. Additionally, Hangzhou Jingtao has been granted a one-time subscription right to invest up to RMB500 million in MHX Technology at the same price per unit of increased registered capital as that under the current subscription agreement, set to be exercised on or before the 31st of December 2026.

The financing is accompanied by two share incentive plans aimed at attracting and retaining key talent within MHX Technology, aligning employee interests with the long-term objectives of the business. This strategic move not only enhances the company’s research and development capabilities but also aims to drive commercialization efforts, enabling Li Auto to realize greater economies of scale.

Sep 22, 2026: Li Auto Ventures into Chip Supply, Competing with Bosch and Huawei

Chinese electric vehicle (EV) startups are collectively pivoting from 'selling cars' to 'selling technology.' On 21st September 2026, Li Auto officially responded to industry rumors, confirming it will externally supply self-developed Mach chips and silicon carbide (SiC) modules, among other components. With this move, all four leading Chinese EV startups—Leapmotor, XPeng, NIO, and Li Auto—have become suppliers of externally exported technology and components, directly encroaching on the turf of traditional automotive suppliers like Bosch and Huawei.

Li Auto explicitly stated it will export Mach chips, SiC modules, and other 'small chips that are easier for other manufacturers to adapt,' but denied the possibility of externally supplying its self-developed battery packs, citing excessive customization that makes standardization difficult. This announcement marks Li Auto's official entry into the technology outsourcing camp, following NIO, XPeng, and Leapmotor. Compared with the other three, Li Auto is the latest entrant, but its chosen entry point is notably strategic—targeting embodied AI companies for differentiated competition rather than going head-to-head with traditional chipmakers in the automotive-grade market.

XPeng is the first among the four to reap tangible rewards from technology outsourcing. In July 2023, Volkswagen acquired a 4.99% stake in XPeng for approximately $700 million, leading to an expanded partnership in various areas, including EV platforms and software. This collaboration has significantly boosted XPeng's financials, with 'services and other revenue' reaching 4.73 billion yuan (approximately $706.4 million) in H1 2026, showcasing the lucrative potential of technology outsourcing compared to traditional vehicle manufacturing, where profit margins are razor-thin.

NIO's technology outsourcing has taken two paths: one through a spin-off of its chip business to establish Anhui Shenji Technology Co., Ltd., which has already begun generating external revenue, and the other through its battery-swap assets. Meanwhile, Leapmotor has opted for a distinct approach, focusing on three-electric technology while forming strategic partnerships with major automotive players. The collective pivot toward technology outsourcing stems from the rapidly compressing profit margins in vehicle manufacturing, with average margins in the segment falling to a ten-year low of 1.5% in H1 2026.

Despite the clear business logic of technology outsourcing, challenges remain. Experts note that technology partnerships may serve as supplementary income with significant uncertainties, including the longevity of partnerships and potential shifts in supplier relationships. Li Auto's choice to enter the embodied AI track with its Mach chips reflects a recognition of the competitive landscape, where established players dominate the automotive-grade chip market. As the automotive industry undergoes a sweeping transformation, the power dynamics within the value chain continue to evolve, with Li Auto positioning itself strategically in the technology outsourcing arena.

Sep 12, 2026: Li Auto Set to Enter European Market Ahead of Schedule

Li Auto Inc. is poised to launch its first vehicle in Europe, the Li 6, at the upcoming Paris Motor Show next month. This marks a significant shift in strategy, as it plans to start selling in Europe in the fourth quarter of this year, a full six years ahead of the timeline previously set by its founder, Li Xiang. The announcement comes on the heels of a competitive automotive landscape in Europe, where multiple Chinese brands, including BYD and XPeng, are also vying for market share.

The Li 6 will be featured prominently at the Paris Motor Show, scheduled for 13th October 2026. However, Li Auto has yet to disclose specific details such as pricing, targeted countries, or sales channels for its European operations. Notably, the company is focusing solely on its battery electric models for this market entry, leaving its extended-range vehicles for markets in the Middle East and Central Asia. This strategic decision aligns with the European Union’s regulations that impose duties on Chinese electric vehicles with range extenders.

The shift away from extended-range vehicles indicates a calculated approach by Li Auto to navigate the complexities of the European automotive market. The EU's duty structure imposes tariffs on vehicles that incorporate range extenders, which generate electricity through gasoline. As a result, only pure battery electric vehicles will be introduced, while the extended-range models will cater to other international markets. This decision reflects not only regulatory considerations but also the current limitations in charging infrastructure across Europe.

Moreover, the company faces uncertainty regarding the tariff rates it will encounter upon entry into the European market. Li Auto is not included among the sampled manufacturers, which may subject it to a range of tariffs from 20.7% to as high as 45.3%, depending on its classification as a cooperating or non-cooperating exporter. Given that the Li 6 starts at approximately $36,000 in China, the added tariffs could position it alongside premium models such as the BMW iX3 in European showrooms, challenging its competitive pricing strategy.

In light of these developments, Li Auto's ambition to establish a foothold in Europe appears both timely and strategic, albeit fraught with challenges. As the company prepares to unveil the Li 6, it remains to be seen how effectively it can capture market share in a continent that is becoming increasingly crowded with electric vehicle offerings from various manufacturers. The upcoming Paris Motor Show will serve as a critical platform for Li Auto to showcase its capabilities and engage with potential customers in this burgeoning market.
